- **chemical-coverage-analysis**: Analyser correctement la couverture chimique d'un groupe de plantes — quelles espèces ont une chimie décrite et pourquoi. À utiliser AVANT toute affirmation du type « ces plantes sont inexplorées », « cette lignée est sous-étudiée » ou « cette région est un angle mort chimique ». Contient les six contrôles qui invalident la plupart de ces affirmations. ([Oshinsu/plant-chemistry-skills](https://github.com/Oshinsu/plant-chemistry-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/chemical-coverage-analysis))
- **chemical-coverage-optimization**: Use when your LC-MS feature table is incomplete or has low chemical coverage because traditional peak extraction algorithms (e.g., standard XCMS workflows) systematically miss features at m/z and retention time positions corresponding to known suspect compounds in your database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/chemical-coverage-optimization) · 2 copies)
- **chemical-cycle-research**: 化工周期与化工产品量化投研分析技能。采用自上而下双轨投研范式：【阶段一：大宗化工全板块横向截面扫描雷达】一键对齐 8 大子板块（纯碱/PTA/MEG/甲醇/PVC/钛白粉/聚氨酯/制冷剂）的吨价差、边际成本击穿深度、库存时钟、海关出口象限与业绩弹性，输出截面综合打分与轮动排序；【阶段二：细分品种垂直深度爆破】解剖单一化工品多工艺成本曲线、供需剪刀差、四阶段库存周期与周期股估值反向时钟。当用户询问化工截面轮动、化工大盘扫描、周期拐点排行、化工品垂直深度研究、细分品类供需与价差、买卖点与标的选择时使用此技能。 ([aidenreed937/stock](https://github.com/aidenreed937/stock/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/chemical-cycle-research) · ★2)
- **chemical-data**: Inspect CAS Common Chemistry contract status and query substance records only when a provider-issued API contract is configured. Use for chemical names, CAS Registry Numbers, structures, and basic compound information; not literature, reaction, or materials-property discovery. ([luffysolution-svg/omnischolar](https://github.com/luffysolution-svg/omnischolar/tree/HEAD/skills/chemical-data) · ★8)
- **chemical-database-candidate-selection**: Use when running VInSMoC on run.npanalysis.org and you must pick which chemical database to search against, or associate an uploaded spectrum file with a project so it can serve as input to a database-search task. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-database-candidate-selection))
- **chemical-database-crosslink-counting**: Use when a database release claims compound structure coverage (e.g. '1188 structures with SMILES', '1347→1860 BGCs with structure data') and you need to reproduce those counts and per-database cross-link tallies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-database-crosslink-counting))
- **chemical-database-curation**: Use when curating or auditing a natural product database where each BGC record has an associated product structure (e.g. MIBiG/PubChem links), and you need to detect entries whose linked SMILES/structure mismatches the biosynthetically predicted product. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-database-curation))
- **chemical-database-curation-audit**: Use when reproducing or verifying aggregate counts of a curated chemical database assembled from multiple public sources, or when reconciling per-source entry totals against published unique-molecule statistics after deduplication. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-database-curation-audit))
- **chemical-database-field-completeness-checking**: Use when a curated chemical/adduct database is distributed as structure files (.mol/.cdxml) plus a spreadsheet, and you must verify identifier columns are populated and reproducible entry-by-entry. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-database-field-completeness-checking))
- **chemical-database-query-and-matching**: Use when when you have mass-to-charge (m/z) values from mass spectrometry imaging or other MS experiments and need to assign molecular formulae with high precision, especially in spatially-resolved metabolomics where traditional LC-MS annotation methods are insufficient. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/chemical-database-query-and-matching) · 3 copies)
- **chemical-database-querying-and-retrieval**: Use when you have BioTransformer-predicted metabolite structures (in SMILES or InChI format) and need to identify which known compounds in public databases match those structures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/chemical-database-querying-and-retrieval) · 2 copies)
- **chemical-database-search-configuration**: Use when raw mass spectra (MGF, mzXML, or mzML) must be searched against a candidate chemical database (e.g., NPAtlas, COCONUT, PubChem) and the VInSMoC task parameters need to be set to match the input spectra's ion mode, precursor m/z, and expected adducts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-database-search-configuration))
- **chemical-database-structure-retrieval**: Use when a predicted Morgan fingerprint bit string from an HSQC spectrum (DeepSAT output) is available and candidates must be retrieved, ranked, and annotated against known-molecule fingerprint databases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-database-structure-retrieval))
- **chemical-dataset-record-counting**: Use when a deposited .sdf dataset must be audited to confirm its record counts match published per-category totals, especially when annotations need deduplication or category classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-dataset-record-counting))
- **chemical-decontamination-filtering**: Use when extracting component areas from a spreadOut()-processed GC-MS hit table and you must purge chemicals that lack strong cross-sample library matches, PubChem records, or canonical SMILES before downstream standardization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-decontamination-filtering))
- **chemical-depiction**: Render validated science-tools molecule state as sanitized static SVG with local RDKit. Use for inline-ready 2D chemical depiction; not for reactions, highlighting, browser chemistry runtimes, or proof of visible Canvas pixels. ([LA3D/linked-science-cloud](https://github.com/LA3D/linked-science-cloud/tree/HEAD/skills/chemical-depiction))
- **chemical-descriptor-extraction**: Use when you have standardized molecular structures (SMILES or SDF format) and need to generate a uniform feature matrix for machine learning models (e.g., retention time prediction). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/chemical-descriptor-extraction))
- **chemical-descriptor-importance-extraction**: Use when a fitted retention time prediction model (e.g. Retip Keras on HILIC or RPLC) needs interpretation: which molecular descriptors most strongly predict RT, with percentage importance weights. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-descriptor-importance-extraction))
- **chemical-descriptor-retrieval**: Use when after harmonizing compound identifiers to PubChem IDs (e.g., via check_names function). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/chemical-descriptor-retrieval))
- **chemical-distillation**: Use when doing distillation. ([LoopyLuci/Skills](https://github.com/LoopyLuci/Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/chemical-distillation) · ★2)
- **chemical-dropout-probability-sampling**: Use when you need synthetic chemical datasets for multiple samples (e.g. control vs case groups) where each chemical is present in only a subset of samples according to a configurable dropout rate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-dropout-probability-sampling))
- **chemical-element-frequency-filtering**: Use when curating McLean CCS Compendium training data for [M-H]-, [M+H]+ or [M+Na]+ adducts and entries containing rare chemical elements (fewer than five occurrences in the database) must be excluded before model training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-element-frequency-filtering))

- **chengqianbihou-zhibing-jiuren**: 何时调用：当用户需要理解不讲情面揭发错误加完全为了救人的建设性纠偏方法时。 关键信号："怎么惩前毖后治病救人？"、"不讲情面揭发错误完全为了救人？"、"建设性纠偏？"。 何时不调用：用户需要的是两类矛盾（用 lianglei-maodun），或用户需要的是组织纠偏诊断（用 zuzhi-jiupian-zhenduan）。 ([jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills](https://github.com/jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills/tree/HEAD/mao-xuan/chengqianbihou-zhibing-jiuren) · ★1)
- **chengqianbihou-zhibing-jiuren**: 不讲情面揭发错误 + 完全为了救人的建设性纠偏方法。当用户需要处理团队成员的严重错误但又不忍心处罚时激活。 不调用场景：对方恶意破坏且不可转化的场景（用两类矛盾skill中的对抗性处理）、小事不需要严肃纠偏。 来源：《毛泽东选集第1-5卷》毛泽东 ([zhangzhimiao1994/--cross-system-hub](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/--cross-system-hub/tree/HEAD/skills/chengqianbihou-zhibing-jiuren) · 6 copies)
